Optimization routine details.

We need efficient score/gradient for LSE. Let's define:
```
def fft_len(M): return 1 << (M-1).bit_length()

def conv_fft(u,v):
   n=len(u); m=len(v); M=n+m-1; L=fft_len(M); U=np.fft.rfft(u,L); V=np.fft.rfft(v,L); return np.fft.irfft(U*V,L)[:M]

def self_conv(a): conv_fft(a,a)
```

```
def corr_a_p(a,p):
 n=len(a); R=2*n-1; M=n+R-1; L=fft_len(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L); P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); h=np.fft.irfft(A*P,L); return h[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Then grad_L =2*b.

For self conv c=a*a: conv a with itself. For normalized or raw. Use helper.

LSE for c: Need stable. c can have negative tiny due FFT; clip to 0? Conv of nonnegative may have tiny negative ~1e-15. For LSE, we can use c = np.maximum(c,0). But max maybe negative if a small; handle.

`lse_max_and_p(c, beta)`:
```
cmax=np.max(c)
if cmax<=0: p uniform? return 0, ones/R/R? 
scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
# To avoid overflow, scaled<=0; but for beta large 1000 and c diff up to maybe ~0.01 ->10 okay. Later diff smaller. Okay.
z=np.exp(scaled)
sumz=z.sum()
p=z/sumz
L=cmax + np.log(sumz)/beta
```
If beta high and many active, p concentrated. If cmax=0, score0? But sum>0, c>0.

Gradient for score raw:
```
def lse_score_grad(a,beta):
 n=len(a); S=sum(a)
 if S<1e-12: return inf, zeros
 c=self_conv(a)
 cmax=max(c); scaled=beta*(c-cmax); p=exp/s
 L=cmax+log(s)/beta
 M=L
 b=corr_a_p(a,p)  # sum a_m p_{i+m}
 grad_L=2*b
 grad = 2*n*(grad_L/(S*S) - 2*M*a/(S**3))
 # If adding sum penalty lmda: grad += 2*lmda*(S-1)*ones
 return 2*n*M/(S*S), grad
```
But for softmax, use normalized a sum1 and score without S denominator:
score=2n L. grad_a=2n*(2*b - 2*L*a)? Wait if a sum1, grad_L=2*b. Score=2n L. d score =2n*2*b? But need gradient wrt a on tangent? Actually if a not constrained to sum1? For normalized a with sum1, and we consider variations preserving sum1, gradient of L wrt a is 2*b. But if we take ordinary gradient of F(a)=2nL(a) (where L smooth max of conv(a,a), not enforcing sum), it's 4n b, no -2L a. The -2L a term was from raw scale-invariant score M/S^2. def as_good(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-12: a=np.ones(len(a))*0.5
    else: a=a/S
    return a
```

C init:
```
def init_sqrt(n, rng=None):
    c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1): c[i+1]=c[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
    c/=c.sum(); return c
```
Loop O(n) okay for up to 32768. Could vectorize cumulative product but loop in Python 30k okay. If many lengths, okay. For speed, maybe use recurrence vectorized with np.cumprod:
```
i=np.arange(1,n); factors=(2*i)/(2*i+1)? Wait C_i = product_{k=0}^{i-1} (2k+1)/(2k+2). For i=0 C0=1. For i>=1, cumulative product of (2k+1)/(2k+2) for k=0..i-1.
factors=(2*np.arange(n-1)+1)/(2*np.arange(n-1)+2); c[1:]=np.cumprod(factors)
```
This is fast. Use float64.

def init_power(n, alpha): i=np.arange(1,n+1); a=np.power(i, -alpha); normalize

def run_lse_lbfgs(a, beta, maxiter, maxfun, sum_penalty=1e-3, bounds_max=1000):
    n=len(a)
    bounds=[(1e-12, bounds_max)]*n  # lower positive? 0 okay but for LSE p? Use 0.

MWA function:
```
def mwa_direction(a, beta=100, eta=1.0, iters=200, q0=None):
    n=len(a); R=2*n-1
    q=a.copy() if q0 is None else q0
    q=q/sum
    p=np.ones(R)/R
    for it in range(iters):
       c=conv_fft(a,q)
       cmax=c.max()
       if cmax<=0: break
       cs=c/cmax
       # p = softmax(beta*cs)
       z=np.exp(beta*(cs-cs.max()))? Since cs max1, scaled=beta*(cs-1) <=0; p=exp/s
       # Actually use p = exp(beta*cs)/sum; stable: shifted = beta*(cs-1)
       z=np.exp(beta*(cs-1.0)); p=z/z.sum()
       b=corr_a_p(a,p)
       bmax=b.max(); if bmax<=0: bmax=1
       bs=b/bmax
       q=np.exp(-eta*bs)
       q=q/q.sum()
    return q
```
Will q become too concentrated? eta=1, bs in [0,1], exp min 0.367, not too. Maybe eta adaptive 2. This finds q with small b. Since p emphasizes high c, b high where a has high correlation with active lags; reducing those shifts mass. Good.

Outer MWA fixed point:
```
def mwa_refine(a, deadline, beta_s list, eta_s, mix):
   current=a
   for beta in [20,50,100,300]:
      for iters chunk time:
         q=mwa_direction(current, beta, eta, iters=?)
         # line search mix
         best=current; bestsc=score
         for t in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
             mix=normalize((1-t)*current + t*q)
             sc=exact_score(mix)
             if sc<bestsc: best=mix
         if bestsc>=score: break
         current=best
   return current
```

At near optimum, score gap small. We can perform coordinate/ascent targeted by active lags. For each active lag k with c_k close to M, we can adjust pairs (i,j) with i+j=k: reducing product and increasing elsewhere. Direct L-BFGS maybe enough, but we can do "pair swaps" preserving sum: choose i (high contributor) and j (low), move small mass δ: a_i decreases, a_j increases. Evaluate score; accept if improves. This is like combinatorial local search; can find improvements L-BFGS misses. With FFT, evaluating full score after each swap O(n log n) too slow for many. But can update convolution incrementally? If moving δ only touches coefficients c_k involving i or j: c_{i+j?} etc. Could do cheap approximate? Maybe not necessary.

Another idea: Since objective is scale-invariant, we can optimize using "normal equations from active lags". If we identify active lags K (where c_k > M - εM), set weights p_k =1/|K| and perform one step of minimizing weighted sum of c_k subject to sum=1. Implementation of `run_lbfgs` with callback:
```
CB_DEADLINE = 0.0

def time_callback(x):
    return time.monotonic() < CB_DEADLINE

def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, deadline, sum_penalty=1e-3, m=20):
    global CB_DEADLINE
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float).copy()
    S=a0.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a0[:]=1.0/len(a0)
    # maybe normalize to sum1 to start (score invariant but penalty)
    a0=a0/S
    n=len(a0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    CB_DEADLINE=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+0.1) # wait deadline absolute
    res=minimize(objective_lbfgs, a0, args=(beta,sum_penalty), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                 options={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30,'m':m},
                 callback=time_callback)
    out=np.clip(res.x,0,1000); S=out.sum(); if S>0: out=out/S
    else out=a0/S
    return exact_score(out), out
```
But `CB_DEADLINE` must be absolute deadline for this call, not e.g. phase slice. We can pass `call_deadline`. In run, set global to `call_deadline`. If callback stops, SciPy returns with x at current. Good. `minimize` might raise? wrap try.

If callback stops early and then `res.x` maybe last. Good.

def lse_score_grad(a,beta,sum_penalty):
    n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
    c=conv_fft(a,a)
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax<=0:
       score=0.0; grad=np.zeros(n)
    else:
       shifted=beta*(c-cmax)
       # shifted <=0, but due beta large min maybe -inf if c-cmax huge? c nonnegative and max 1000? difference up to 1e6? beta maybe 10000 -> -1e10 underflow okay. Need prevent exp of positive due numerical: shifted = np.minimum(shifted,0)
       z=np.exp(shifted)
       sumz=z.sum()
       if sumz<=0: p=np.full(len(c),1/len(c)); L=cmax
       else: p=z/sumz; L=cmax+np.log(sumz)/beta
       b=corr_a_p(a,p)
       S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
       score=2.0*n*L/S2 + sum_penalty*(S-1)*(S-1)
       grad=(4.0*n*b/S2 - 4.0*n*L*a/S3)
       if sum_penalty:
          grad += 2.0*sum_penalty*(S-1)*np.ones(n)
    return score, grad
```
Wait if cmax=0 but S>0 impossible? If a has one element? If n=1, c0=a^2>0. okay.
Potential overflow in p: shifted min 0; exp(0)=1. sumz ≤R. Good. `np.log(sumz)` okay. For beta large and c differences small, p near uniform over active? Actually scaled=β(c-cmax), active c=cmax ->1; near cmax -δ -> exp(-βδ). If β large, only ties. Good.

LP direction via L-BFGS:
```
def lp_score_grad(x,a,beta,sum_penalty):
    n=len(x) # assume len a=n
    S=x.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return 1e12, zeros
    c=conv_fft(a,x)  # linear conv length 2n-1
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    cmax=c.max()
    shifted=beta*(c-cmax); z=...
    L=cmax+log(sumz)/beta
    p=z/sumz
    b=corr_a_p(a,p) # derivative wrt x sum a_m p_{i+m}
    score=L + sum_penalty*(S-1)^2
    grad=b + 2*sum_penalty*(S-1)ones
    return score, grad

def lp_objective(x,a,beta,sum_penalty): return lp_score_grad(x,a,beta,sum_penalty)
```

If sum_penalty too large, objective dominated by sum constraint; gradient 2*10*(S-1)=20, LSE gradient O? L ~M~1e-4, gradient b ~? b_i ~p weighted a; p sum1, b ~O(1/n)? Actually c=conv(a,q), max~M~1e-4. LSE gradient b = weighted sum a, magnitude ~M? b_i ≤S? Could be ~1e-4. Penalty gradient 20 dominates, okay for sum but may make LP direction just uniform sum1? def try_mix(current, candidate, current_score, steps=(0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0)):

       S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12: continue
       a=a/S
       sc=exact_score(a)

Let's detail MWA direction implementation with deadline inside:
```
def mwa_direction(a, beta, eta, max_iters, deadline):
    n=len(a); q=a.copy(); S=q.sum(); if S>0 q/=S
    p=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1))
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       c=conv_fft(a,q)
       cmax=c.max()
       if cmax<=1e-300: break
       cs=c/cmax
       shifted=beta*(cs-1.0)
       z=np.exp(shifted)
       sz=z.sum()
       if sz<=0: p=np.full(len(c),1.0/len(c))
       else: p=z/sz
       b=corr_a_p(a,p)
       bmax=b.max()
       if bmax<=0: break
       bs=b/bmax
       # Use eta maybe min(3, beta?); choose 1.0
       q=np.exp(-eta*bs)
       S=q.sum()
       if S<=0: break
       q/=S
    return q
```
`cs` max exactly1; due cmax, some cs close 1. shifted <=0.
If beta=0? not.

Targeted perturbation:
```
def targeted_perturb(a, strength):
   n=len(a); c=self_conv(a); k=int(c.argmax())
   # contribution b to c[k] = a_{j} for j range; reduce top contributors
   j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
   contrib=np.zeros(n)
   if j_end>=j_start:
      contrib[j_start:j_end+1]=a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1] # a[k-j]
   top=max(3,n//50)
   top_idx=np.argsort(contrib)[-top:]
   low_idx=np.argsort(contrib)[:-top]
   ap=a.copy(); total=0
   for idx in top_idx: reduce=ap[idx]*strength; ap[idx]-=reduce; total+=reduce
   if total>0 and len(low_idx)>0: ap[low_idx]+= total/len(low_idx)
   return normalize(ap)
```
Could improve active lag. Use few times with L-BFGS.

Random perturb:
```
def random_perturb(a,rng,strength):
   ap=a*(1+strength*rng.standard_normal(len(a))); return normalize(np.maximum(ap,1e-9))
```
Use strengths 0.02,0.05,0.1.

Subgradient polish with high beta gradient and small steps:
```
def grad_polish(a, iterations, beta, deadline):
   best=a; best_sc=exact_score(a)
   cur=a.copy(); S=cur.sum(); cur/=S
   lb = 1.0 + 1/(2*n-1) # lower bound
   for it in range(iterations):
      if time>deadline break
      sc, grad = lse_score_grad(cur,beta,0.0) # penalty 0
      # grad scale invariant; for normalized cur sum1. take grad_t = grad - (cur*grad).sum()/cur? no.

Let's scrutinize LP/MWA update direction mathematically to avoid wrong sign. For fixed a, we solve min_q max_k a*q. We update q_i ∝ exp(-η b_i) where b=A^T p, p weights high a*q rows. Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

CB_DEADLINE = 0.0

def time_callback(x):
    return time.monotonic() < CB_DEADLINE

def fft_len(M):
    if M <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (M-1).bit_length()

def conv_fft(x,y):
    x=np.asarray(x,float); y=np.asarray(y,float)
    M=len(x)+len(y)-1
    L=fft_len(M)
    if L<=1: return np.array([x[0]*y[0] if M>0 else 0])
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L); Y=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(X*Y,L)
    return out[:M]

def self_conv(a): return conv_fft(a,a)

def corr_a_p(a,p):
    n=len(a); R=2*n-1
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    M=n+R-1
    L=fft_len(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(A*P,L)
    return h[n-1:2*n-1]

def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=1e-300:
       a=np.ones_like(a)*0.5
       S=a.sum()
    a = a/S
    return a

def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-300: return np.inf
    c=self_conv(a)
    M=float(c.max())
    if M<0: M=0.0
    return float(2.0*len(a)*M/(S*S))
```
`normalize` scales sum always 1. If a length 0? not.

def init_sqrt(n):
    c=np.ones(n)
    if n>1:
       idx=np.arange(1,n)
       factors=(2*idx-? ) Need formula C_i for i>=1. For i from1 to n-1, factor from C_{i-1} to C_i = (2(i-1)+1)/(2(i-1)+2) = (2i-1)/(2i).
       factors=(2*np.arange(1,n)-1)/(2*np.arange(1,n))
       c[1:]=np.cumprod(factors)
    return normalize(c)
```

LBFGS objective:
```
def lse_score_grad(a, beta, sum_penalty):
    n=len(a)
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S<1e-300:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
    c=conv_fft(a,a)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        score = sum_penalty*S*S? if sum_penalty? Not needed.
        return score, np.zeros(n)
    shifted = beta*(c-cmax)
    np.minimum(shifted, 0.0, out=shifted)
    z=np.exp(shifted)
    sumz=float(z.sum())
    if sumz <=0:
       p=np.full(c.shape,1.0/c.size); L=cmax
    else:
       p=z/sumz; L=cmax+np.log(sumz)/beta
    b=corr_a_p(a,p)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    score=2.0*n*L/S2
    grad=(4.0*n*b/S2 - 4.0*n*L*a/S3)
    if sum_penalty !=0.0:
       score += sum_penalty*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
       grad += 2.0*sum_penalty*(S-1.0)  # broadcasts
    return float(score), grad
```
Broadcast adds scalar to gradient? `grad += scalar` works adding to all elements. Good.
If sum_penalty small but S drifts, gradient penal.

`objective_lbfgs(x,beta,sum_penalty): return lse_score_grad(x,beta,sum_penalty)`.

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, deadline, sum_penalty=1e-3, m=20):
    a0=normalize(a0)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return exact_score(a0), a0
    n=len(a0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    global CB_DEADLINE
    CB_DEADLINE=float(deadline)
    options={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'maxls':30,'m':int(m)}
    try:
       res=minimize(objective_lbfgs, a0, args=(float(beta), float(sum_penalty)), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options, callback=time_callback)
       out=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    except Exception:
       out=a0
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(out.sum())
    if S>1e-300: out=out/S
    else: out=a0
    return exact_score(out), out
```
`m` maybe `max(5,min(50,n//?))`; pass 20.

LP objective:
```
def lp_objective(x, a, beta, sum_penalty):
    n=len(x)
    S=float(x.sum())
    if S<1e-300: return 1e12, np.zeros(n)
    c=conv_fft(a, x)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax<=0: return sum_penalty*(S-1)**2, zeros
    shifted=beta*(c-cmax); np.minimum(shifted,0,out=shifted); z=np.exp(shifted); sumz=z.sum(); p=z/sumz; L=cmax+log(sumz)/beta
    b=corr_a_p(a,p)  # note a fixed, p length 2n-1
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    score=L + sum_penalty*(S-1)^2
    grad=b + 2*sum_penalty*(S-1)
    return float(score), grad
```
Is derivative of LSE of c=conv(a,x) wrt x exactly b? c_k=∑_{i} a_i x_{k-i}. ∂c_k/∂x_i = a_{k-i} if k-i in [0,n-1]. Sum p_k a_{k-i} = b_i. Good. Need p length R. `corr_a_p(a,p)` returns b_i. Good.
But if `a` and `x` both length n, conv length 2n-1. a fixed sum1, x sum~1.

def run_lp(a, beta, maxiter, maxfun, deadline, sum_penalty=0.1):
    a=normalize(a); x0=a.copy()
    if time.monotonic()>=deadline: return x0
    n=len(a); bounds=[(0.0,10.0)]*n
    global CB_DEADLINE; CB_DEADLINE=deadline
    options={...}
    try: res=minimize(lp_objective, x0, args=(a,beta,sum_penalty), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options, callback=time_callback)
    except: q=x0
    q=np.clip(res.x,0,10); S=q.sum(); if S>0 q/=S else q=x0
    return q
```

MWA:
```
def mwa_direction(a, beta, eta, max_iters, deadline=None):
    a=normalize(a)
    n=len(a); R=2*n-1
    q=a.copy()
    p=np.full(R, 1.0/R)
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       c=conv_fft(a,q)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax<=1e-300: break
       cs=c/cmax
       shifted=beta*(cs-1.0)
       shifted=np.minimum(shifted,0.0)
       z=np.exp(shifted); sz=z.sum()
       if sz<=0: p=np.full(R,1.0/R)
       else: p=z/sz
       b=corr_a_p(a,p)
       b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
       bmean=float(b.mean())
       if bmean>1e-300:
           factor=np.exp(-float(eta)*(b/bmean - 1.0))
           q = q*factor
           S=q.sum()
           if S<=0: break
           q=q/S
       else: break
    return q
```
Could factor overflow if b/bmean -1 large positive/negative. b nonnegative, min 0 => -1, max maybe large; exp(-eta*large) underflow okay, max factor when min 0 => e^eta. If bmean small, b/bmean huge; underflow. Use clip arg to [-50,50]:
```
arg = -eta*(b/bmean -1.0); arg=np.clip(arg,-50,50); factor=np.exp(arg)
```
Similarly p shifted clipped: shifted already <=0; if beta*(cs-1) maybe -1e6 underflow okay but no overflow; no positive.

Line search mix:
```
def mix_line_search(a, cand, current_score, steps=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.6,1.0)):
    best_score=current_score; best_a=a
    cand=normalize(cand)
    for t in steps:
       tt=float(t)
       if tt<=0: continue
       mix=(1-tt)*a + tt*cand
       S=mix.sum()
       if S<1e-300: continue
       mix=mix/S
       sc=exact_score(mix)
       if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_a=mix
    return best_score, best_a
```
Assumes a normalized, cand normalized. Good.

Targeted perturb top-level:
```
def targeted_perturb(a, strength):
   a=normalize(a); n=len(a); c=self_conv(a); k=int(c.argmax())
   j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
   contrib=np.zeros(n)
   if j_end>=j_start:
      contrib[j_start:j_end+1]=a[j_start:j_end+1][::-1]
   top=max(3,n//50)
   # If n small, top < n
   if top >= n: top=max(1,n-1)
   order=np.argsort(contrib)
   top_idx=order[-top:]
   low_idx=order[:n-top]
   ap=a.copy(); total=0
   for idx in top_idx:
      red=ap[idx]*float(strength)
      if red<=0: continue
      ap[idx]-=red; total+=red
   if total>0 and low_idx.size>0:
      ap[low_idx] += total/low_idx.size
   return normalize(ap)
```
Random:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    a=normalize(a)
    ap=a*(1.0+float(strength)*rng.standard_normal(len(a)))
    return normalize(np.maximum(ap,1e-9))
```

`subgrad_polish` maybe top-level with `lse_score_grad`:
```
def subgrad_polish(a, deadline, iterations=300, beta=5000):
    a=normalize(a); n=len(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    cur=a.copy()
    lb=1.0 + 1.0/(2.0*n-1.0)
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(iterations):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       sc, grad = lse_score_grad(cur, beta, 0.0)
       # remove mean along a to ensure tangent
       # grad should already scale invariant; but due numerical, do grad -= np.sum(cur*grad) (since norm? For tangent, subtract (grad·cur) * ones? If sum constraint: project gradient to simplex: g_t = grad - (grad·cur) * cur? Wait if direction in tangent space satisfies sum d=0. For raw gradient of scale-invariant f, a·grad=0. For log update, use raw grad. For Euclidean projected, d = grad - (1/n)sum grad? Not.
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gn<1e-14: break
       step=(sc-lb)/(gn*gn+1e-30)
       step=min(step,0.05)
       cur = cur - step*grad
       # if negative, clip and redistribute? Use multiplicative safer:
       # cur_log = np.log(cur+1e-30) - step*grad; cur=softmax?
       cur=np.maximum(cur,0.0)
       S=cur.sum()
       if S<1e-30: break
       cur=cur/S
       sc=exact_score(cur)
       if sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=cur; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>50: step*=0.5
       if no_improve>200: break
    return best_sc,best_a
```

# raw grad tangent; use a_i *= exp(-alpha * grad_i / max|grad|)

Top-level:
```
def resample_seq(a,n):
    a=normalize(a); m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize(np.maximum(b,0))
```

weights_list=[frac for jobs]

Subgradient implementation details: use multiplicative update with LSE gradient and step. Need avoid making score worse drastically. Since we only keep best, okay. But if it does no improvement, waste. Limit time.
```
def subgrad_polish(a, deadline, iterations, beta):
    a=normalize(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a); cur=a.copy(); n=len(a)
    for it in range(iterations):
       if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       sc, grad=lse_score_grad(cur, beta, 0.0)
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if gmax<1e-15: break
       # small step
       step=0.02
       cur=cur*np.exp(-step*grad/gmax)
       S=cur.sum(); if S<1e-12: break; cur/=S
       sc=exact_score(cur)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=cur.copy()
       # if not improve many, reduce step
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Gradient may be zero along sum, exp multiplies changes sum; normalize. Good.

Resample top-level:
```
def resample_seq(a, n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); m=len(a)
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)*0.5
   if m==n: return normalize(a)
   xold=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); xnew=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   b=np.interp(xnew,xold,a)
   return normalize(np.maximum(b,0.0))
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(30.0,float(budget_s))
    deadline=t0+budget-15.0
    if deadline <= t0+10: deadline=t0+budget
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    print(f"Start budget={budget}")
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if p.size >= 256:
          p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
          if p.size > 13000:
             p=resample_seq(p,10240)
          S=float(p.sum())
          if S>1e-12:
             parent=p/S
             sc=exact_score(parent)
             print(f"Parent n={parent.size} score={sc}")
             best_sc,best_a=update_with_score(best_sc,best_a,sc,parent,"parent")
          else: parent=None
    except Exception as e:
       print("No parent:", e)
```

Potential issue: In `lp_objective`, if cmax=0 but sum x positive? Could happen if a all zero? a normalized so not. But if c from conv(a,x) with x zeros? no.

Potential issue: `update_with_score` uses exact_score if sc not passed? We'll pass.

Let's construct code carefully.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Key ideas used here:
- Use the positive square-root-of-rectangle series as a strong structured
  initializer: coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n.
- Optimize the scale-invariant objective with LSE-smoothed max-convolution
  under L-BFGS-B, using beta continuation and strict time callbacks.
- Use a mirror-descent/MWA direction for the fixed-F LP surrogate
  min_q max_k (F*q)_k, and mix it into the current sequence by line search.
- Add targeted/local perturbations and a final polishing phase.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# Global deadline for scipy L-BFGS callbacks.
CB_DEADLINE = 0.0


def time_callback(x):
    """Stop scipy iteration when the global deadline is reached."""
    return time.monotonic() < CB_DEADLINE


def fft_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def conv_fft(x, y):
    """Linear convolution via real FFT."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    m = x.size + y.size - 1
    if m <= 0:
        return np.asarray([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = fft_len(m)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    Y = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(X * Y, L)
    return out[:m]


def self_conv(a):
    """Linear self-convolution."""
    return conv_fft(a, a)


def corr_a_p(a, p):
    """
    For a length n and p length 2n-1, return b of length n with
        b_i = sum_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j * p_{i+j}.
    This is the gradient coupling for linear convolutions.
    """
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray([], dtype=np.float64)
    r = 2 * n - 1
    m = n + r - 1
    L = fft_len(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A * P, L)
    return h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_seq(a):
    """Clip to non-negative finite values and normalize sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
        s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def exact_score(a):
    """Exact internal score, matching evaluate_sequence after clipping/normalization."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = self_conv(a)
    if c.size:
        m = float(c.max())
    else:
        m = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    val = 2.0 * float(a.size) * m / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf")
    return float(val)


def update_with_score(best_score, best_a, score, a, label):
    """Update best if a finite lower score is found."""
    if a is None:
        return best_score, best_a
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return best_score, best_a
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return best_score, best_a
    if score < best_score:
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        print(f"  NEW BEST  score={score:.9f}  n={a.size:6d}  {label}")
        return score, a
    return best_score, best_a


def sqrt_raw(n):
    """First n coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), unnormalized."""
    n = int(n)
    c = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
        factors = (2.0 * idx - 1.0) / (2.0 * idx)
        c[1:] = np.cumprod(factors)
    return c


def init_sqrt(n):
    """Positive square-root initializer, normalized sum 1."""
    return normalize_seq(sqrt_raw(int(n)))


def init_sqrt_tilt(n, tilt):
    """Square-root initializer with exponential tilt to bias mass left/right."""
    c = sqrt_raw(int(n))
    w = np.exp(float(tilt) * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n))
    return normalize_seq(c * w)


def init_power(n, alpha):
    """Power-law initializer a_i ~ (i+1)^(-alpha)."""
    i = np.arange(1, int(n) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(i, -float(alpha))
    return normalize_seq(a)


def resample_seq(a, n):
    """Linearly resample a sequence to length n and normalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if a.size == 0:
        return normalize_seq(np.ones(n))
    if a.size == n:
        return normalize_seq(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_seq(np.maximum(b, 0.0))


def lse_score_grad(a, beta, sum_penalty):
    """
    LSE-smoothed scale-invariant score and gradient with optional sum penalty.
    Score: 2*n*L / S^2, where L is LSE-smoothed max of convolve(a,a).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.asarray([])

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = self_conv(a)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        base_score = 0.0
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        shifted = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
        np.minimum(shifted, 0.0, out=shifted)
        z = np.exp(shifted)
        zsum = float(z.sum())
        if zsum <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / max(1, c.size), dtype=np.float64)
            L = cmax
        else:
            p = z / zsum
            L = cmax + np.log(zsum) / float(beta)

        b = corr_a_p(a, p)
        b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

        s2 = s * s
        s3 = s2 * s
        base_score = 2.0 * float(n) * L / s2
        grad = (4.0 * float(n) * b / s2) - (4.0 * float(n) * L * a / s3)

    score = base_score
    if sum_penalty != 0.0:
        score += float(sum_penalty) * (s - 1.0) * (s - 1.0)
        grad = grad + (2.0 * float(sum_penalty) * (s - 1.0))

    score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return score, grad


def objective_lbfgs(x, beta, sum_penalty):
    return lse_score_grad(x, beta, sum_penalty)


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, deadline, sum_penalty=1e-3, m=20):
    """Run L-BFGS-B on the LSE smooth score until deadline."""
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline) - 0.5:
        return exact_score(a0), a0

    n = a0.size
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    global CB_DEADLINE
    CB_DEADLINE = float(deadline)

    options = {
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 30,
        "m": int(m),
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            objective_lbfgs,
            a0,
            args=(float(beta), float(sum_penalty)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
            callback=time_callback,
        )
        out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        out = a0.copy()

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        out = out / s
    else:
        out = a0.copy()
    return exact_score(out), out


def lp_objective(x, a_fixed, beta, sum_penalty):
    """
    Surrogate LP subproblem:
        minimize LSE(convolve(a_fixed, x)) + penalty(sum(x)-1)^2
    over x >= 0 by L-BFGS.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a_fixed = np.asarray(a_fixed, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e12, np.asarray([])

    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = conv_fft(a_fixed, x)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        score = float(sum_penalty) * (s - 1.0) * (s - 1.0)
        return score, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    shifted = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    np.minimum(shifted, 0.0, out=shifted)
    z = np.exp(shifted)
    zsum = float(z.sum())
    if zsum <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / max(1, c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = z / zsum
        L = cmax + np.log(zsum) / float(beta)

    b = corr_a_p(a_fixed, p)
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

    score = float(L)
    grad = b.copy()
    if sum_penalty != 0.0:
        score += float(sum_penalty) * (s - 1.0) * (s - 1.0)
        grad = grad + (2.0 * float(sum_penalty) * (s - 1.0))

    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return score, grad


def run_lp_direction(a0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, deadline, sum_penalty=1e-2):
    """
    Compute a direction q by solving (approximately) the fixed-f LP surrogate.
    """
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline) - 0.5:
        return a0

    n = a0.size
    x0 = a0.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 10.0)] * n
    global CB_DEADLINE
    CB_DEADLINE = float(deadline)

    options = {
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 20,
        "m": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lp_objective,
            x0,
            args=(a0, float(beta), float(sum_penalty)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
            callback=time_callback,
        )
        out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        out = x0.copy()

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        out = out / s
    else:
        out = x0.copy()
    return out


def mwa_direction(a0, beta, eta, max_iters, deadline=None):
    """
    Mirror-weight / multiplicative update direction for
        min_{q sum=1, q>=0} max_k (convolve(a0, q))_k.
    """
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a0.size
    r = 2 * n - 1
    q = a0.copy()
    p = np.full(r, 1.0 / max(1, r), dtype=np.float64)

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
            break

        c = conv_fft(a0, q)
        c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        cs = c / cmax
        shifted = float(beta) * (cs - 1.0)
        np.minimum(shifted, 0.0, out=shifted)
        z = np.exp(shifted)
        zsum = float(z.sum())
        if zsum <= 0.0:
            p = np.full(r, 1.0 / max(1, r), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = z / zsum

        b = corr_a_p(a0, p)
        b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

        bmean = float(b.mean())
        if bmean <= 1e-300:
            break

        arg = -float(eta) * (b / bmean - 1.0)
        arg = np.clip(arg, -50.0, 50.0)
        factor = np.exp(arg)
        q = q * factor
        q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        q = q / s

    return q


def mix_line_search(a, candidate, current_score):
    """Line search between a and candidate, accepting only improvements."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    candidate = normalize_seq(candidate)
    best_score = float(current_score)
    best_a = a.copy()

    for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 1.0):
        mix = (1.0 - t) * a + t * candidate
        s = float(mix.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        mix = mix / s
        sc = exact_score(mix)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_a = mix.copy()
    return best_score, best_a


def targeted_perturb(a0, strength):
    """Reduce high contributors near one max-conv lag and add mass elsewhere."""
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 2:
        return a0.copy()

    c = self_conv(a0)
    k = int(c.argmax())
    j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
    j1 = min(k, n - 1)
    contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if j1 >= j0:
        contrib[j0:j1 + 1] = a0[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]

    top_count = max(3, n // 50)
    top_count = min(top_count, max(1, n - 1))
    order = np.argsort(contrib)
    top_idx = order[-top_count:]
    low_idx = order[:n - top_count]

    ap = a0.copy()
    total = 0.0
    for idx in top_idx:
        red = float(strength) * ap[idx]
        if red > 0.0:
            ap[idx] -= red
            total += red
    if total > 0.0 and low_idx.size > 0:
        ap[low_idx] += total / float(low_idx.size)

    return normalize_seq(ap)


def random_perturb(a0, rng, strength):
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    ap = a0 * (1.0 + float(strength) * rng.standard_normal(a0.size))
    ap = np.maximum(ap, 1e-12)
    return normalize_seq(ap)


def subgrad_polish(a0, deadline, iterations=80, beta=10000.0):
    """Safe multiplicative LSE-subgradient polish; keeps best only."""
    a0 = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = a0.size
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    cur = a0.copy()
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(iterations)):
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
            break
        _, grad = lse_score_grad(cur, float(beta), 0.0)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if gmax <= 1e-15:
            break

        step = 0.02
        arg = -step * grad / gmax
        arg = np.clip(arg, -50.0, 50.0)
        cur = cur * np.exp(arg)
        cur = np.nan_to_num(cur, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        cur = np.maximum(cur, 0.0)
        s = float(cur.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break
        cur = cur / s

        sc = exact_score(cur)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = cur.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 40:
                step *= 0.5
        if stall > 150:
            break

    return best_sc, best_a


def sanitize_output(a):
    """Make output list valid for evaluate_sequence."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return [0.5] * 64
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if a.size > 16000:
            a = resample_seq(a, 10240)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            return [0.5] * 64
        a = a / s
        return [float(v) for v in a]
    except Exception:
        return [0.5] * 64


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.
    Returns the best non-negative sequence found inside the time budget.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 15.0
    if deadline <= t0 + 10.0:
        deadline = t0 + budget

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = None

    print(f"propose_candidate start: budget={budget:.0f}s")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Load parent if available.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 256:
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            if p.size > 13000:
                p = resample_seq(p, 10240)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                parent = p / s
                sc = exact_score(parent)
                print(f"Parent loaded: n={parent.size}, score={sc:.9f}")
                best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, parent, "parent")
            else:
                parent = None
    except Exception as e:
        print("Parent unavailable:", e)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Build a diversified set of jobs. Each job is optimized by
    # L-BFGS with beta continuation, plus extra high-beta polishing.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    jobs = []

    if parent is not None:
        jobs.append((parent, [30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0], 0.30, "parent"))

    # Root square-root initializers.
    for n0 in (8192, 10240):
        if parent is not None and parent.size == n0:
            # Avoid duplicating the parent length entirely, but still keep a root
            # with smaller weight because this basin is important.
            a = init_sqrt(n0)
            sc = exact_score(a)
            best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"root{n0}")
            jobs.append((a, [20.0, 60.0, 200.0, 700.0], 0.05, f"root{n0}_dup"))
        else:
            a = init_sqrt(n0)
            sc = exact_score(a)
            best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"root{n0}")
            jobs.append((a, [20.0, 60.0, 200.0, 700.0, 2000.0, 6000.0], 0.18, f"root{n0}"))

    # Tilted root: biases the decreasing square-root profile.
    a = init_sqrt_tilt(8192, -1.5)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, "tilt8192")
    jobs.append((a, [50.0, 150.0, 500.0, 1500.0], 0.07, "tilt8192"))

    # Resampled parent into a larger grid.
    if parent is not None and parent.size != 10240:
        a = resample_seq(parent, 10240)
        sc = exact_score(a)
        best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, "resample_parent_10240")
        jobs.append((a, [30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0], 0.06, "resample_parent_10240"))

    # A more gradual power-law alternative.
    a = init_power(8192, 0.48)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, "power8192")
    jobs.append((a, [20.0, 60.0, 200.0, 700.0], 0.04, "power8192"))

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = init_sqrt(8192)
        best_score = exact_score(best_a)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Dynamic allocation of the main optimization phase.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    main_deadline = deadline - max(90.0, 0.12 * (deadline - t0))
    weights = [float(j[2]) for j in jobs]
    if sum(weights) <= 0.0:
        jobs = []
        weights = []

    for idx, (a0, betas, w, label) in enumerate(jobs):
        rem_w = float(sum(weights[idx:]))
        if rem_w <= 0.0:
            continue
        alloc = float(w) / rem_w
        remaining_to_main = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        job_time = remaining_to_main * alloc
        if job_time < 15.0 and remaining_to_main > 60.0:
            job_time = max(job_time, 15.0)
        job_end = min(main_deadline, time.monotonic() + job_time)

        if time.monotonic() >= job_end:
            continue

        print(f"  Job {label}: allocate {job_time:.0f}s until {job_end - t0:.0f}s")

        a = normalize_seq(a0)
        sc = exact_score(a)
        best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, label)

        # Beta continuation.
        for bi, beta in enumerate(list(betas)):
            if time.monotonic() >= job_end:
                break
            remaining = job_end - time.monotonic()
            if remaining <= 2.0:
                break
            # Divide remaining job time among remaining stages, with a small floor.
            stages_left = len(betas) - bi
            stage_time = max(5.0, remaining / float(stages_left))
            stage_deadline = min(job_end, time.monotonic() + stage_time)
            sc, a = run_lbfgs(
                a,
                beta,
                maxiter=10000,
                maxfun=200000,
                deadline=stage_deadline,
                sum_penalty=1e-3,
                m=20,
            )
            best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"{label}/beta{beta:g}")
            if a is None:
                break

        # Extra high-beta polishing within this job's allocation.
        extra_beta = max(float(max(betas)), 1.0) * 3.0
        while time.monotonic() < job_end - 2.0 and a is not None:
            use_time = min(job_end, time.monotonic() + max(15.0, 0.25 * (job_end - time.monotonic())))
            if use_time <= time.monotonic():
                break
            sc, a = run_lbfgs(
                a,
                extra_beta,
                maxiter=5000,
                maxfun=100000,
                deadline=use_time,
                sum_penalty=1e-4,
                m=20,
            )
            best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"{label}/extra{extra_beta:g}")
            if not np.isfinite(best_score) or best_a is None:
                break
            extra_beta = min(80000.0, extra_beta * 3.0)
            if extra_beta >= 50000.0 and time.monotonic() + 30.0 >= job_end:
                break

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = init_sqrt(8192)
        best_score = exact_score(best_a)

    best_a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    print(f"  After main jobs: best={best_score:.9f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final refinement phase:
    #   1) high-beta L-BFGS polishing
    #   2) mirror-descent / MWA directions with line search
    #   3) local perturbations + small L-BFGS
    #   4) subgradient polish
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, deadline - 5.0)

    # High-beta L-BFGS polishing.
    for beta in (5000.0, 15000.0, 50000.0):
        if time.monotonic() >= final_deadline - 20.0:
            break
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 20.0:
            break
        use = min(final_deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + min(90.0, 0.18 * rem))
        if use <= time.monotonic():
            continue
        sc, a = run_lbfgs(
            best_a,
            beta,
            maxiter=12000,
            maxfun=240000,
            deadline=use,
            sum_penalty=1e-4,
            m=25,
        )
        best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"final_lbfgs_beta{beta:g}")
        if best_a is None:
            break

    # Mirror / MWA directions.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 40.0:
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        mwa_end = min(final_deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.40 * rem))
        betas_mwa = [300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0, 10000.0]
        counter = 0
        while time.monotonic() < mwa_end and counter < 24:
            beta = betas_mwa[counter % len(betas_mwa)]
            counter += 1
            iters = 35 if best_a.size > 10000 else 70
            ww = min(mwa_end, time.monotonic() + 18.0)
            q = mwa_direction(best_a, beta, eta=1.0, max_iters=iters, deadline=ww)
            sc, a = mix_line_search(best_a, q, best_score)
            best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"mwa_beta{beta:g}")
            if best_a is None:
                break

    # LP-d.direction + local perturbations.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 50.0:
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        local_end = min(final_deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.35 * rem))
        trials = 0
        while time.monotonic() < local_end and trials < 14:
            trials += 1
            if trials % 4 == 1:
                cand = targeted_perturb(best_a, 0.02 + 0.04 * rng.random())
                label = "targeted"
                use_beta = 2000.0
                use_iter = 3000
            elif trials % 4 == 2:
                cand = random_perturb(best_a, rng, 0.04 + 0.10 * rng.random())
                label = "random"
                use_beta = 1500.0
                use_iter = 3000
            elif trials % 4 == 3:
                qw = mwa_direction(best_a, 3000.0, 1.0, 30, deadline=min(local_end, time.monotonic() + 15.0))
                cand = qw
                label = "mwa_direct"
                use_beta = None
                use_iter = 0
            else:
                qw = run_lp_direction(
                    best_a,
                    beta=5000.0,
                    maxiter=1200,
                    maxfun=25000,
                    deadline=min(local_end, time.monotonic() + 30.0),
                    sum_penalty=1e-2,
                )
                cand = qw
                label = "lp_direct"
                use_beta = None
                use_iter = 0

            if use_beta is None:
                sc, a = mix_line_search(best_a, cand, best_score)
            else:
                stage = min(local_end - 2.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
                sc, a = run_lbfgs(
                    cand,
                    use_beta,
                    maxiter=use_iter,
                    maxfun=60000,
                    deadline=stage,
                    sum_penalty=1e-3,
                    m=20,
                )
            best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, f"local_{label}_{trials}")
            if best_a is None:
                break

    # Final subgradient polish.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 8.0:
        sc, a = subgrad_polish(best_a, deadline=final_deadline, iterations=100, beta=20000.0)
        best_score, best_a = update_with_score(best_score, best_a, sc, a, "subgrad_polish")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final validation and return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = init_sqrt(8192)
        best_score = exact_score(best_a)

    out = sanitize_output(best_a)

    verified = float("inf")
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception as e:
        print("verify error:", e)
        verified = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        out = [0.5] * 64
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = float("inf")

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal score = {best_score:.9f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified score = {verified:.9f}")
    print(f"FINAL length = {len(out)}, elapsed = {time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
